# Zepto Support Assistant

An offline-first RAG service for Zepto policy questions. `MOCK_LLM` is enabled by default: leave it unset (or set `MOCK_LLM=1`) to make the complete, deterministic flow run without an API key or an LLM network call.

## Run locally

```bash
cd support_assistant
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 7860
```

The first request loads the local `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` sentence-transformers model and creates the persistent Chroma database in `.chroma/`. The Docker build preloads those model weights so serving needs no model-provider network call. In an intentionally air-gapped developer machine where the weights were never cached, the app uses a deterministic token-vector contingency so the mock demonstration remains runnable; normal local/Docker usage uses MiniLM.

```bash
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7860/ask -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"query":"What is the delivery fee?"}'
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7860/ask -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"query":"What is the capital of France?"}'
```

Example mock-mode JSON responses (the first snippet is deterministically drawn from the top retrieved chunk):

```json
{"answer":"Based on the retrieved context: Zepto delivers grocery and household essentials to serviceable pin codes within 10 to 30 minutes of order confirmation, depending on the customer's delivery zone and current order volume. Standard del","sources":["doc_01","doc_04","doc_03"],"confidence":1.0}
```

```json
{"answer":"I can only answer questions about Zepto policies right now.","sources":[],"confidence":1.0}
```

## Terminal output snapshot

```bash
$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7860/ask \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query":"When is Zepto standard delivery free?"}' | python -m json.tool
{
  "answer": "Based on the retrieved context: Zepto delivers grocery and household essentials within 10 to 30 minutes of order confirmation, depending on zone and load. Standard delivery is free on orders over INR 149; orders below this threshold incur a flat INR 25 delivery fee.",
  "sources": [
    "doc_01",
    "doc_04",
    "doc_03"
  ],
  "confidence": 1.0
}

$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7860/ask \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query":"What is the capital of France?"}' | python -m json.tool
{
  "answer": "I can only answer questions about Zepto policies right now.",
  "sources": [],
  "confidence": 1.0
}
```

## Architecture

```text
docs/doc_01.txt ... doc_08.txt
       | ingestion + per-document chunking (PolicyStore._ingest_if_needed)
       v
all-MiniLM-L6-v2 local embeddings --> Chroma collection: zepto_policy_chunks
       |                                         |
POST /ask --> classify_intent --> policy_question --> retrieve_and_answer --> JSON
                  |                    (top-3 cosine retrieval)                 
                  +--> general_question --> direct_answer ---------------------->
```

1. **Ingestion:** `PolicyStore._ingest_if_needed` reads the eight corpus files; each complete document is one chunk with its `doc_XX` ID.
2. **Embedding:** that same component uses the local `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` model and persists the vectors in Chroma collection `zepto_policy_chunks` with cosine similarity.
3. **Retrieval:** the LangGraph `retrieve_and_answer` node embeds a policy query and fetches the top three chunks from Chroma.
4. **Generation:** `classify_intent` routes to `retrieve_and_answer` or `direct_answer`. In mock mode, they construct deterministic answers and Pydantic validates `answer`, `sources`, and `confidence`. The optional real path uses the actual role–context–task–format–length prompt in `prompt.py`, including a negative constraint and few-shot example. Invalid real-LLM JSON is retried twice with a correction before returning a marked error response.

Every generation point branches on `MOCK_LLM`: default mock mode uses the specified keyword classifier, retrieved-context template, and fixed direct response without LLM calls. With `MOCK_LLM=0`, the optional Groq-compatible implementation makes real LLM calls (requiring `GROQ_API_KEY`); embedding and Chroma retrieval remain local in both modes.

## Docker

```bash
cd support_assistant
docker build -t zepto-support .
docker run --rm -p 7860:7860 zepto-support
```

Then send the same `POST /ask` calls to `http://127.0.0.1:7860/ask`. The image serves mock mode by default.

2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis project is a complete mini data pipeline built for catalog-style pricing and availability analysis. It scrapes live product data from \u003ca href=\"https://books.toscrape.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ebooks.toscrape.com\u003c/a\u003e, cleans the scraped values, converts prices from GBP to INR using a fixed project rate, stores the data in a normalized SQLite database, and then analyzes it using both SQL and pandas.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe website uses books as the product catalog, but the pipeline is similar to what a company could use for competitive pricing, product availability checks, and catalog benchmarking.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Live website -\u0026gt; Scraper -\u0026gt; Cleaned data -\u0026gt; SQLite database -\u0026gt; SQL queries -\u0026gt; pandas validation\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eLive website -\u0026gt; Scraper -\u0026gt; Cleaned data -\u0026gt; SQLite database -\u0026gt; SQL queries -\u0026gt; pandas validation\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" \u003ccode\u003ebooks.db\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003equeries.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRuns the required scraper.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eScrapes and cleans the live book data, then creates \u003ccode\u003escraped_books.csv\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epython3 database.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCreates normalized SQLite tables and saves the cleaned records into \u003ccode\u003ebooks.db\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epython3 queries.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRuns SQL queries and prints the results in the terminal.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epython3 analysis.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReads SQL results with pandas and checks SQL JOIN output against pandas merge output.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp categories:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eCategory\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth align=\"right\"\u003eBooks align=\"right\"\u003e32\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYoung Adult\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd align=\"right\"\u003e54\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTotal records 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe scraper does more than collect raw text. It prepares the data so it can be used safely in analysis.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eField\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eCleaning Logic\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003etitle\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStripped and kept as text.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice_gbp\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExtracted from price text and converted to \u003ccode\u003efloat\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003erating\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eConverted from text classes like \u003ccode\u003eOne\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eTwo\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eThree\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eFour\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eFive\u003c/code\u003e into integers from 1 to 5.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ein_stock\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eConverted into a boolean value, then stored in SQLite as \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecategory\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStored separately in the \u003ccode\u003ecategories\u003c/code\u003e table to keep the database normalized.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice_inr\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCalculated from \u003ccode\u003eprice_gbp\u003c/code\u003e using the fixed baseline conversion rate.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf a numeric value such as price or rating fails to parse, the code uses median imputation. Rows missing essential fields like \u003ccode\u003etitle\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003ecategory\u003c/code\u003e are dropped because they cannot be reliably used as catalog records.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFixed Currency Rate\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca rate:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"1 GBP = 105.50 INR\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e1 GBP = 105.50 INR\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe conversion formula is:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"price_inr = price_gbp * 105.50\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice_inr = price_gbp * 105.50\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis is not a live exchange rate. It is a project-defined constant, so no currency API or date reference is required.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSQLite Database Design\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-sqlite-database-design\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: SQLite Database Design\" href=\"#sqlite-database-design\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe database is normalized into two related tables:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"categories\nbooks\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecategories\nbooks\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003ecategories\u003c/code\u003e table stores category names once. The \u003ccode\u003ebooks\u003c/code\u003e table stores book-level details and uses \u003ccode\u003ecategory_id\u003c/code\u003e as a foreign key.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-sql notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"CREATE TABLE categories (\n    category_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    category_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE books (\n    book_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title TEXT NOT NULL,\n    price_gbp REAL NOT NULL,\n    price_inr REAL NOT NULL,\n    rating INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    in_stock INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    category_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    scraped_at TEXT,\n    FOREIGN KEY (category_id)\n        REFERENCES categories(category_id)\n);\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eCREATE\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eTABLE\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ecategories\u003c/span\u003e (\n    category_id \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eINTEGER\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ePRIMARY KEY\u003c/span\u003e,\n    category_name \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eTEXT\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e UNIQUE\n);\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eCREATE\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eTABLE\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ebooks\u003c/span\u003e (\n    book_id \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eINTEGER\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ePRIMARY KEY\u003c/span\u003e AUTOINCREMENT,\n    title \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eTEXT\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    price_gbp \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eREAL\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    price_inr \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eREAL\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    rating \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eINTEGER\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    in_stock \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eINTEGER\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    category_id \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eINTEGER\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eNOT NULL\u003c/span\u003e,\n    scraped_at \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eTEXT\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eFOREIGN KEY\u003c/span\u003e (category_id)\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eREFERENCES\u003c/span\u003e categories(category_id)\n);\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis design avoids repeating category names for every book and satisfies the primary key and foreign key requirement.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" higher\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSELECT\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eWHERE\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eORDER BY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTen cheapest books\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eORDER BY\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eLIMIT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDistinct categories\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eDISTINCT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBooks priced between 20 and 40 GBP\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eBETWEEN\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBooks from Mystery or Travel\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eIN\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eJOIN\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTop 10 highest-rated books with category\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eJOIN\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eORDER BY\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eLIMIT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese queries help answer practical catalog questions such as which products are highly rated, which products are cheapest, which categories exist, and how book records connect to category separately.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRecreates the JOIN using \u003ccode\u003epd.merge(...)\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe script then compares both outputs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExpected result:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" aper.py","contentType":"file"}],"totalCount":9},"":{"items":[{"name":"datapipeline","path":"datapipeline","contentType":"directory"}],"totalCount":1}},"fileTreeProcessingTime":15.600286,"foldersToFetch":[]},"codeViewBlobLayoutRoute":{"codeLineWrapEnabled":false,"refInfo":{"name":"feature/datapipeline","listCacheKey":"v0:1786662833.0","canEdit":true,"refType":"branch","currentOid":"822e98f3041347194e0ce2bfe328a4798e81bb5a","canEditOnDefaultBranch":false,"fileExistsOnDefault":false},"path":"datapipeline/README.md","blob":{"copilotSWEAgentEnabled":false,"dependabotInfo":{"showConfigurationBanner":false,"configFilePath":null,"networkDependabotPath":"/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project/network/updates","dismissConfigurationNoticePath":"/settings/dismiss-notice/dependabot_configuration_notice","configurationNoticeDismissed":false},"displayName":"README.md","displayUrl":"https://github.com/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project/blob/feature/datapipeline/datapipeline/README.md?raw=true","headerInfo":{"blobSize":"6.92 file","ghDesktopPath":"x-github-client://openRepo/https://github.com/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project?branch=feature%2Fdatapipeline\u0026filepath=datapipeline%2FREADME.md","isGitLfs":false,"onBranch":true,"shortPath":"bb351a0","siteNavLoginPath":"/login?return_to=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Framsaij158-pixel%2Fzepto_captone_project%2Fblob%2Ffeature%2Fdatapipeline%2Fdatapipeline%2FREADME.md","isCSV":false,"isRichtext":true,"lineInfo":{"truncatedLoc":"236","truncatedSloc":"170"},"mode":"file"},"image":false,"isCodeownersFile":null,"isPlain":false,"isValidLegacyIssueTemplate":false,"isIssueTemplate":false,"isDiscussionTemplate":false,"language":"Markdown","languageID":222,"large":false,"planSupportInfo":{"repoIsFork":null,"repoOwnedByCurrentUser":null,"requestFullPath":"/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project/blob/feature/datapipeline/datapipeline/README.md","showFreeOrgGatedFeatureMessage":null,"showPlanSupportBanner":null,"upgradeDataAttributes":null,"upgradePath":null},"publishBannersInfo":{"dismissActionNoticePath":"/settings/dismiss-notice/publish_action_from_dockerfile","releasePath":"/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project/releases/new?marketplace=true","showPublishActionBanner":false},"rawBlobUrl":"https://github.com/ramsaij158-pixel/zepto_captone_project/raw/refs/heads/feature/datapipeline/datapipeline/README.md","renderImageOrRaw":false,"shortPath":null,"symbolsEnabled":true,"tabSize":4,"topBannersInfo":{"overridingGlobalFundingFile":false,"globalPreferredFundingPath":null,"showInvalidCitationWarning":false,"citationHelpUrl":"https://docs.github.com/github/creating-cloning-and-archiving-repositories/creating-a-repository-on-github/about-citation-files","actionsOnboardingTip":null},"truncated":false,"viewable":true,"workflowRedirectUrl":null},"copilotInfo":null,"copilotAccessAllowed":true,"copilotSpacesEnabled":true,"modelsAccessAllowed":false,"modelsRepoIntegrationEnabled":false,"isMarketplaceEnabled":true},"codeViewBlobLayoutRoute.StyledBlob":{"rawLines":["## Project Overview","","This project is a complete mini data pipeline built for catalog-style pricing and availability analysis. It scrapes live product data from [books.toscrape.com](https://books.toscrape.com/), cleans the scraped values, converts prices from GBP to INR using a fixed project rate, stores the data in a normalized SQLite database, and then analyzes it using both SQL and pandas.","","The website uses books as the product catalog, but the pipeline is similar to what a company could use for competitive pricing, product availability checks, and catalog benchmarking.","","```text","Live website -\u003e Scraper -\u003e Cleaned data -\u003e SQLite database -\u003e SQL queries -\u003e pandas validation","```","","## Folder Contents","","| File | Purpose |","| --- | --- |","| `scraper.py` | Scrapes book data from the website, cleans it, converts prices, and saves `scraped_books.csv`. |","| `database.py` | Creates the SQLite database schema and loads cleaned book data into `books.db`. |","| `queries.py` | Runs the required SQL queries and prints the query results. |","| `analysis.py` | Uses pandas `read_sql` and `merge` to compare SQL JOIN output with pandas output. |","| `requirements.txt` | Lists the Python packages required to run the project. |","| `books.db` | SQLite database file created by `database.py`. |","| `scraped_books.csv` | CSV file created by `scraper.py`. |","| `README.md` | Documentation for setup, run steps, schema, and design decisions. |","","## Install","","Open a terminal in this project folder:","","```bash","cd /Users/ramsaijeevan001/Documents/datapipeline","```","","Install the required packages:","","```bash","python3 -m pip install -r requirements.txt","```","","The `requirements.txt` file contains:","","```text","requests","beautifulsoup4","pandas","```","","## Run The Pipeline","","Run the project files in this order:","","```bash","python3 scraper.py","python3 database.py","python3 queries.py","python3 analysis.py","```","","What happens during each step:","","| Command | Output |","| --- | --- |","| `python3 scraper.py` | Scrapes and cleans the live book data, then creates `scraped_books.csv`. |","| `python3 database.py` | Creates normalized SQLite tables and saves the cleaned records into `books.db`. |","| `python3 queries.py` | Runs SQL queries and prints the results in the terminal. |","| `python3 analysis.py` | Reads SQL results with pandas and checks SQL JOIN output against pandas merge output. |","","Because `books.db` is already included, you can skip scraping and loading if you only want to see the query results:","","```bash","python3 queries.py","python3 analysis.py","```","","## Data Source","","The data is scraped from:","","```text","https://books.toscrape.com/","```","","The scraper collects books from these three categories:","","| Category | Books Collected |","| --- | ---: |","| Travel | 11 |","| Mystery | 32 |","| Young Adult | 54 |","","Total records collected:","","```text","97 books","```","","Each scraped book contains:","","```text","title","price_gbp","rating","in_stock","category","```","","After cleaning and conversion, the final dataset also includes:","","```text","price_inr","```","","## Cleaning And Conversion","","The scraper does more than collect raw text. It prepares the data so it can be used safely in analysis.","","| Field | Cleaning Logic |","| --- | --- |","| `title` | Stripped and kept as text. |","| `price_gbp` | Extracted from price text and converted to `float`. |","| `rating` | Converted from text classes like `One`, `Two`, `Three`, `Four`, `Five` into integers from 1 to 5. |","| `in_stock` | Converted into a boolean value, then stored in SQLite as `1` or `0`. |","| `category` | Stored separately in the `categories` table to keep the database normalized. |","| `price_inr` | Calculated from `price_gbp` using the fixed baseline conversion rate. |","","If a numeric value such as price or rating fails to parse, the code uses median imputation. Rows missing essential fields like `title` or `category` are dropped because they cannot be reliably used as catalog records.","","## Fixed Currency Rate","","This project uses the required fixed baseline rate:","","```text","1 GBP = 105.50 INR","```","","The conversion formula is:","","```text","price_inr = price_gbp * 105.50","```","","This is not a live exchange rate. It is a project-defined constant, so no currency API or date reference is required.","","## SQLite Database Design","","The database is normalized into two related tables:","","```text","categories","books","```","","The `categories` table stores category names once. The `books` table stores book-level details and uses `category_id` as a foreign key.","","```sql","CREATE TABLE categories (","    category_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,","    category_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE",");","","CREATE TABLE books (","    book_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,","    title TEXT NOT NULL,","    price_gbp REAL NOT NULL,","    price_inr REAL NOT NULL,","    rating INTEGER NOT NULL,","    in_stock INTEGER NOT NULL,","    category_id INTEGER NOT NULL,","    scraped_at TEXT,","    FOREIGN KEY (category_id)","        REFERENCES categories(category_id)",");","```","","This design avoids repeating category names for every book and satisfies the primary key and foreign key requirement.","","## SQL Queries","","`queries.py` runs six SQL queries. The assignment asks for at least five, so this project includes one extra query for stronger coverage.","","| Query | SQL Feature Covered |","| --- | --- |","| Available books with rating 4 or higher | `SELECT`, `WHERE`, `ORDER BY` |","| Ten cheapest books | `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT` |","| Distinct categories | `DISTINCT` |","| Books priced between 20 and 40 GBP | `BETWEEN` |","| Books from Mystery or Travel | `IN`, `JOIN` |","| Top 10 highest-rated books with category | `JOIN`, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT` |","","These queries help answer practical catalog questions such as which products are highly rated, which products are cheapest, which categories exist, and how book records connect to category records.","","## pandas Validation","","`analysis.py` proves that the database JOIN result can also be reproduced with pandas.","","It performs three important steps:","","1. Reads a JOIN query result using `pd.read_sql(...)`.","2. Loads `books` and `categories` separately.","3. Recreates the JOIN using `pd.merge(...)`.","","The script then compares both outputs.","","Expected result:","","```text","SQL JOIN output and pandas merge output match.","```","","## Verified Output","","The pipeline was tested successfully.","","```text","Saved 97 books to books.db","Mystery: 32","Travel: 11","Young Adult: 54","SQL JOIN output and pandas merge output match.","```","","## Final Submission Checklist","","This folder includes all required parts of the module:","","- Live scraping with `requests` and `BeautifulSoup`","- Cleaning and type conversion with pandas","- Fixed GBP to INR conversion using `105.50`","- Normalized SQLite database with primary key and foreign key","- More than five SQL queries","- Required SQL clauses: `WHERE`, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT`, `DISTINCT`, `IN`, `BETWEEN`, and `JOIN`","- pandas `pd.read_sql(...)`","- pandas `pd.merge(...)`","- README documentation with install steps, run steps, schema, and design decisions","","## Short Summary","","This data pipeline starts with live catalog data and ends with a queryable SQLite database plus pandas validation. It is small, readable, and complete enough to show the full flow of a real data-engineering BlobContent-module__markdownBlob__T8jpG" data-hpc="true" containertiming="hpc"><article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project Overview</h2><a id="user-content-project-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project Overview" href="#project-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project is a complete mini data pipeline built for catalog-style pricing and availability analysis. It scrapes live product data from <a href="https://books.toscrape.com/" rel="nofollow">books.toscrape.com</a>, cleans the scraped values, converts prices from GBP to INR using a fixed project rate, stores the data in a normalized SQLite database, and then analyzes it using both SQL and pandas.</p>
<p dir="auto">The website uses books as the product catalog, but the pipeline is similar to what a company could use for competitive pricing, product availability checks, and catalog benchmarking.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Live website -&gt; Scraper -&gt; Cleaned data -&gt; SQLite database -&gt; SQL queries -&gt; pandas validation"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Live website -&gt; Scraper -&gt; Cleaned data -&gt; SQLite database -&gt; SQL queries -&gt; pandas validation
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Folder Contents</h2><a id="user-content-folder-contents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Folder Contents" href="#folder-contents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>File</th>
<th>Purpose</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>scraper.py</code></td>
<td>Scrapes book data from the website, cleans it, converts prices, and saves <code>scraped_books.csv</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>database.py</code></td>
<td>Creates the SQLite database schema and loads cleaned book data into <code>books.db</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>queries.py</code></td>
<td>Runs the required SQL queries and prints the query results.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>analysis.py</code></td>
<td>Uses pandas <code>read_sql</code> and <code>merge</code> to compare SQL JOIN output with pandas output.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>requirements.txt</code></td>
<td>Lists the Python packages required to run the project.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>books.db</code></td>
<td>SQLite database file created by <code>database.py</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>scraped_books.csv</code></td>
<td>CSV file created by <code>scraper.py</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>README.md</code></td>
<td>Documentation for setup, run steps, schema, and design decisions.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

beautifulsoup4
pandas"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>requests
beautifulsoup4

</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run The Pipeline</h2><a id="user-content-run-the-pipeline" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run The Pipeline" href="#run-the-pipeline"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run the project files in this order:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python3 scraper.py
python3 database.py
python3 queries.py
python3 analysis.py"><pre>python3 scraper.py
python3 database.py
python3 queries.py
python3 analysis.py</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">What happens during each step:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Command</th>
<th>Output</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>python3 scraper.py</code></td>
<td>Scrapes and cleans the live book data, then creates <code>scraped_books.csv</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>python3 database.py</code></td>
<td>Creates normalized SQLite tables and saves the cleaned records into <code>books.db</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>python3 queries.py</code></td>
<td>Runs SQL queries and prints the results in the terminal.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>python3 analysis.py</code></td>
<td>Reads SQL results with pandas and checks SQL JOIN output against pandas merge output.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Because <code>books.db</code> is already included, you can skip scraping and loading if you only want to see the query results:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python3 queries.py
python3 analysis.py"><pre>python3 queries.py
python3 analysis.py</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Data Source</h2><a id="user-content-data-source" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Data Source" href="#data-source"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The data is scraped from:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="https://books.toscrape.com/"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>https://books.toscrape.com/
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The scraper collects books from these three categories:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Category</th>
<th align="right">Books Collected</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Travel</td>
<td align="right">11</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Mystery</td>
<td align="right">32</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Young Adult</td>
<td align="right">54</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Total records collected:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="97 books"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>97 books
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Each scraped book contains:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="title
price_gbp
rating
in_stock
category"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>title
price_gbp
rating
in_stock
category
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">After cleaning and conversion, the final dataset also includes:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="price_inr"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>price_inr
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Cleaning And Conversion</h2><a id="user-content-cleaning-and-conversion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cleaning And Conversion" href="#cleaning-and-conversion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The scraper does more than collect raw text. It prepares the data so it can be used safely in analysis.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Cleaning Logic</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>title</code></td>
<td>Stripped and kept as text.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>price_gbp</code></td>
<td>Extracted from price text and converted to <code>float</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>rating</code></td>
<td>Converted from text classes like <code>One</code>, <code>Two</code>, <code>Three</code>, <code>Four</code>, <code>Five</code> into integers from 1 to 5.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>in_stock</code></td>
<td>Converted into a boolean value, then stored in SQLite as <code>1</code> or <code>0</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>category</code></td>
<td>Stored separately in the <code>categories</code> table to keep the database normalized.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>price_inr</code></td>
<td>Calculated from <code>price_gbp</code> using the fixed baseline conversion rate.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">If a numeric value such as price or rating fails to parse, the code uses median imputation. Rows missing essential fields like <code>title</code> or <code>category</code> are dropped because they cannot be reliably used as catalog records.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Fixed Currency Rate</h2><a id="user-content-fixed-currency-rate" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Fixed Currency Rate" href="#fixed-currency-rate"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project uses the required fixed baseline rate:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="1 GBP = 105.50 INR"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>1 GBP = 105.50 INR
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The conversion formula is:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="price_inr = price_gbp * 105.50"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>price_inr = price_gbp * 105.50
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This is not a live exchange rate. It is a project-defined constant, so no currency API or date reference is required.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">SQLite Database Design</h2><a id="user-content-sqlite-database-design" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SQLite Database Design" href="#sqlite-database-design"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The database is normalized into two related tables:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="categories
books"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>categories
books
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>categories</code> table stores category names once. The <code>books</code> table stores book-level details and uses <code>category_id</code> as a foreign key.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-sql notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="CREATE TABLE categories (
    category_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    category_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

CREATE TABLE books (
    book_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    price_gbp REAL NOT NULL,
    price_inr REAL NOT NULL,
    rating INTEGER NOT NULL,
    in_stock INTEGER NOT NULL,
    category_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    scraped_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (category_id)
        REFERENCES categories(category_id)
);"><pre><span class="pl-k">CREATE</span> <span class="pl-k">TABLE</span> <span class="pl-en">categories</span> (
    category_id <span class="pl-k">INTEGER</span> <span class="pl-k">PRIMARY KEY</span>,
    category_name <span class="pl-k">TEXT</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span> UNIQUE
);

<span class="pl-k">CREATE</span> <span class="pl-k">TABLE</span> <span class="pl-en">books</span> (
    book_id <span class="pl-k">INTEGER</span> <span class="pl-k">PRIMARY KEY</span> AUTOINCREMENT,
    title <span class="pl-k">TEXT</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    price_gbp <span class="pl-k">REAL</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    price_inr <span class="pl-k">REAL</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    rating <span class="pl-k">INTEGER</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    in_stock <span class="pl-k">INTEGER</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    category_id <span class="pl-k">INTEGER</span> <span class="pl-k">NOT NULL</span>,
    scraped_at <span class="pl-k">TEXT</span>,
    <span class="pl-k">FOREIGN KEY</span> (category_id)
        <span class="pl-k">REFERENCES</span> categories(category_id)
);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This design avoids repeating category names for every book and satisfies the primary key and foreign key requirement.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">SQL Queries</h2><a id="user-content-sql-queries" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SQL Queries" href="#sql-queries"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>queries.py</code> runs six SQL queries. The assignment asks for at least five, so this project includes one extra query for stronger coverage.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Query</th>
<th>SQL Feature Covered</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Available books with rating 4 or higher</td>
<td><code>SELECT</code>, <code>WHERE</code>, <code>ORDER BY</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Ten cheapest books</td>
<td><code>ORDER BY</code>, <code>LIMIT</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Distinct categories</td>
<td><code>DISTINCT</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Books priced between 20 and 40 GBP</td>
<td><code>BETWEEN</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Books from Mystery or Travel</td>
<td><code>IN</code>, <code>JOIN</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Top 10 highest-rated books with category</td>
<td><code>JOIN</code>, <code>ORDER BY</code>, <code>LIMIT</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">These queries help answer practical catalog questions such as which products are highly rated, which products are cheapest, which categories exist, and how book records connect to category records.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">pandas Validation</h2><a id="user-content-pandas-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: pandas Validation" href="#pandas-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>analysis.py</code> proves that the database JOIN result can also be reproduced with pandas.</p>
<p dir="auto">It performs three important steps:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Reads a JOIN query result using <code>pd.read_sql(...)</code>.</li>
<li>Loads <code>books</code> and <code>categories</code> separately.</li>
<li>Recreates the JOIN using <code>pd.merge(...)</code>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">The script then compares both outputs.</p>
<p dir="auto">Expected result:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SQL JOIN output and pandas merge output match."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>SQL JOIN output and pandas merge output match.
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Verified Output</h2><a id="user-content-verified-output" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Verified Output" href="#verified-output"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The pipeline was tested successfully.</p>

Mystery: 32

SQL JOIN output and pandas merge output match."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Saved 97 books to books.db

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Final Submission Checklist</h2><a id="user-content-final-submission-checklist" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Final Submission Checklist" href="#final-submission-checklist"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This folder includes all required parts of the module:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Live scraping with <code>requests</code> and <code>BeautifulSoup</code></li>
<li>Cleaning and type conversion with pandas</li>
<li>Fixed GBP to INR conversion using <code>105.50</code></li>
<li>Normalized SQLite database with primary key and foreign key</li>
<li>More than five SQL queries</li>
<li>Required SQL clauses: <code>WHERE</code>, <code>ORDER BY</code>, <code>LIMIT</code>, <code>DISTINCT</code>, <code>IN</code>, <code>BETWEEN</code>, and <code>JOIN</code></li>
<li>pandas <code>pd.read_sql(...)</code></li>
<li>pandas <code>pd.merge(...)</code></li>
<li>README documentation with install steps, run steps, schema, and design decisions</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Short Summary</h2><a id="user-content-short-summary" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Short Summary" href="#short-summary"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This data pipeline starts with live catalog data and ends with a queryable SQLite database plus pandas validation. It is small, readable, and complete enough to show the full flow of a real data-engineering task.</p>

      .user-mention[href$="/ramsaij158-pixel"] {
        color: var(--color-user-mention-fg);
        background-color: var(--bgColor-attention-muted, var(--color-attention-subtle));
        border-radius: 2px;
        margin-left: -2px;
        margin-right: -2px;
      }
      .user-mention[href$="/ramsaij158-pixel"]:before,
      .user-mention[href$="/ramsaij158-pixel"]:after {
        content: '';
        display: inline-block;
        width: 2px;
      }
    </style>
